**Status**: in-progress  
**Complexity**: M  
**Surface scope**: web  
**Depends on**: Slice 09  
**Implementation gate**: Slice 09 active implementation is complete at 1235/1235 under owner-ratified DEC-132, as recorded in its current tracker. Slice 12 is unblocked by that implementation dependency but remains in progress at 0/296. AC209, AC211, AC265 and AC266 remain separate authored, unchecked external gates on their declared timelines; this correction does not clear or waive them. The completed 1235/1235 proof includes the amended 17 activation-chain criteria plus AC019, AC039, AC043, AC054, AC100, AC143, AC181, AC196, AC215, AC222, AC259, AC264, AC273 and the 956 DEC-108/109/110/111/119/120 criteria AC284-AC1239.  
**Spec depth floor**: 296  
**Acceptance criteria**: 296  
**Plan source**: [Phase 2 plan](../../../wiki/specs/phases/phase-2.md)

**Receiving scope (2026-10-03, DEC-121, DEC-122 owner-ratified and DEC-123)**: (1) Slice 09 AC1166 and AC005 cover only the declaration and storage of `no_fallback` (declared per locale variant, refused on a nonlocalizable field at authoring). The resolution semantics, that a `no_fallback` field is never resolved through `fallbackChains` to `defaultLocale`, and the DEC-121 rule that a stale fallback-permitted field keeps serving its last approved translation, are received by this slice's explicit receiving criteria P2-S12-AC-051 and AC-052 and by Slice 15 delivery resolution (CMS-15). (2) Slice 09 delivers only the template-binding mechanics DEC-123 fixes: a new type carries no template, and CMS-03A-09 binds the default template and bindings through the first successor version, each resolved through the compatibility resolver against the exact candidate. Template authoring and compatibility declaration (CMS-03C-01), compatibility maintenance and every other template-binding flow are received by this slice's explicit receiving criterion P2-S12-AC-053 and Slice 15. All three receiving criteria were authored 2026-10-03 and are open; no Slice 09 criterion verifies the resolution semantics or the flows beyond DEC-123.

**Receiving scope (2026-10-17, DEC-202)**: Owner approved the documentation corrections and eleven explicit checks for initial default-template bootstrap. P2-S12-AC-286–AC-296 are all unchecked; existing AC001–AC285 and AC053 successor-binding scope are unchanged. SQL/Web implementation remains open for TDD; authorization, completed replay and immutable history are preserved.

SPEC GAP: BE § Data Flow — synchronous source-stale fanout has no per-entry
locale bound — an adversary can make one source write exceed its 15-second
deadline by creating many dependent locales — owner to select a bounded
locale count or an asynchronous fail-closed invalidation protocol.

SPEC GAP: BE § Event schemas — distinct locale invalidations can share one
entry `aggregateVersion` — a consumer that deduplicates only by aggregate
version may miss a locale — specify event-ID-plus-locale reconciliation or a
different per-locale aggregate key before implementing the consumer.

- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-051** — Resolve a `no_fallback` field only from its own target-locale variant: it is never resolved through `fallbackChains` to `defaultLocale` or any other locale, and a missing target-locale value for a `no_fallback` field blocks publication of that locale and increments `cms_no_fallback_block_total` (DEC-121; received from Slice 09 AC005 and AC1166 under DEC-122, which own only the declaration and storage); resolved by CMS-03C-20 (shared locale field resolution and locale preflight provider, BE03c ledger 25.05.03). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Middleware & Policies, Observability, Testing Strategy; [IA03](../../../wiki/specs/ia/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Data Models, Edge Cases
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-052** — Keep serving the last approved translation for a stale fallback-permitted locale field after its source changes (never resolving it through the fallback chain to the default locale and never blocking the locale), expose the stale state to editorial reads and `cms_locale_stale_total` until revalidation, and keep `no_fallback` fields blocking publication when their value is missing (DEC-121, DEC-122). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Middleware & Policies, Observability, Testing Strategy; [IA03](../../../wiki/specs/ia/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Data Models, Edge Cases
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-053** — Author template versions that declare compatibility with an existing content-type id through CMS-03C-01 `compatibleTypeIds` and keep every template binding consistent afterwards: a successor that binds a template through CMS-03A-09 resolves it through the compatibility resolver against the exact candidate, refuses an absent template with 404, a template that does not list the type id with 422 and a pointer, and a withdrawn template with 409, and never alters an already-active definition hash (DEC-123; the flows beyond the first-successor binding mechanics that Slice 09 delivers, received under DEC-122). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Middleware & Policies, Observability, Testing Strategy; [BE03a](../../../wiki/specs/be/03a-content-schema-registry.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts, Error Handling
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-054** — CMS-03C-04 registers POST /api/v1/cms/entries/{entryId}/locales/{locale}/variants with the strict `LocaleVariantRequest` (`entryId`, `locale`, `sourceRevisionId`, 1-128 `fields` of `fieldId` and `value`, `fallbackChain` of at most 16 tags, `noFallbackFieldIds` of at most 128, `sourceHash`, `expectedVersion`) and returns the strict `LocaleVariantResource` as 201 (`state` one of `untranslated`, `draft`, `review`, `approved`, `stale`; `entryId`, `revisionId`, `locale`, `sourceRevisionId`, `fallbackChain`, `noFallbackFieldIds` and `staleFieldIds`, which is empty unless `state` is `stale`); one transaction appends the target revision, its field values and the variant, the entry version, the audit record and the `cms.localization.changed.v1` outbox event; generated OpenAPI and the discovered Hono route match the registry row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Transaction and external seams, Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-055** — CMS-03C-04 derives session and acting context server-side and serves only an assigned CMS author or editor who may edit the entry for the localizable fields of the requested locale, on a source revision the caller can read: a hidden or absent entry, locale or source revision is 404 `LOCALE_SOURCE_NOT_FOUND`, a visible entry without edit assignment is 403 `LOCALE_FORBIDDEN`, no ownership, party or person identifier is serialized, and the operation never borrows another jurisdiction's text. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Per-operation authorization matrix, Normative application error catalog, Registry invariants, Permission, RLS and grants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-056** — CMS-03C-04 requires an `Idempotency-Key` and the strong `If-Match` equal to the entry aggregate `version` (`expectedVersion`), applies CAS on the source revision and the entry, enforces a unique entry, locale and source revision, rate buckets of 60/min/user and 120/min/party, a 15,000 ms deadline, no-store and the Tier 2 p95 < 1,200 ms and RPC < 300 ms targets, replays an exact repeat of the same command, and answers a changed actor, body, path or version under the same key with 409. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Registry invariants, Shared Contract Inheritance, Observability
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-057** — CMS-03C-04 maps malformed IDs, header or body (400 `INVALID_REQUEST`), a missing session (401), a denied edit (403 `LOCALE_FORBIDDEN`), a hidden or absent entry or source (404 `LOCALE_SOURCE_NOT_FOUND`), a stale source hash or entry version or a duplicate locale (409 `LOCALE_VERSION_CONFLICT`), a non-JSON body (415), a BCP 47, field, fallback or no_fallback schema failure (422 `LOCALE_VALIDATION_FAILED`), the locale limit (429), a schema or RPC deadline (502/503/504 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`) and an internal failure (500) to typed ApiError of catalog `cms.03c.locale.v1`; a failure before commit leaves no record and a lost response replays by idempotency key. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Contract and error matrix, Normative application error catalog, Operation error coverage
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-058** — CMS-03C-04 is consumed by the protected `CmsLocaleVariantForm` at `/app/cms-content-modeling/entries/:entryId/locales/:locale`, which offers only localizable fields, shows the fallback order read-only from the protected version detail and submits it unchanged as the equality expectation, shows per-field resolution (own value, fallback source locale, `no_fallback` missing or stale) and the derived variant state, marks `staleFieldIds`, never displays another locale's text for a `no_fallback` field, offers explicit revalidation, and renders fixed copy then refetches on 409 `FALLBACK_CHAIN_MISMATCH` and `locale_limit_reached`; verification covers per-field resolution and the `no_fallback` rule, the derived variant states, both fixed 409 messages, the 403/404 split and the focused result heading. [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6), Page and Route Definitions, Interaction Specification, Testing Obligations; [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-059** — CMS-03C-05 registers POST /api/v1/cms/entries/{entryId}/related-content with the strict `RelatedContentRuleRequest` (`entryId`, unique `pins` of at most 32, unique `exclusions` of at most 64, a nullable `derivedRule` of `key`, `version`, `reasonCode` and `maxCandidates`, and `expectedVersion`) and returns the strict `RelatedContentResource` as 201 (`state` `active` or `revoked`, `sourceEntryId`, `pins`, `exclusions`, `derivedRule` of `key` and `version` or null, and `eligibleCount` 0-128); generated OpenAPI and the discovered Hono route match the registry row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-060** — CMS-03C-05 serves only an assigned CMS author or editor who can edit the source entry: a hidden or absent source is 404 `RELATED_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND`, a hidden, absent or ineligible target is the uniform 422 `related_target_ineligible`, a visible source without edit assignment is 403 `RELATED_CONTENT_FORBIDDEN`, a recommendation never grants access to a target the caller could not already read, and the stored rule carries no authority. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Per-operation authorization matrix, Normative application error catalog, Permission, RLS and grants, Security and abuse controls
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-061** — CMS-03C-05 requires an `Idempotency-Key` and the strong `If-Match` equal to the source entry aggregate `version` (`expectedVersion`), applies CAS on the source entry, enforces unique pin and exclusion targets, rate buckets of 60/min/user and 120/min/party, a 15,000 ms deadline, no-store and Tier 2, appends rows without updating any, and emits no event of its own (it consumes `cms.publication.changed.v1` for invalidation). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Registry invariants, Canonical records and fields, Transaction and external seams
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-062** — CMS-03C-05 maps malformed IDs, header or body (400), a missing session (401), a denied source edit (403 `RELATED_CONTENT_FORBIDDEN`), a hidden source (404 `RELATED_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND`), a stale version or duplicate pin (409 `RELATED_CONTENT_VERSION_CONFLICT`), a non-JSON body (415), a pin, exclusion or rule schema failure or a hidden, absent or ineligible target (422 `RELATED_CONTENT_VALIDATION_FAILED`), the relationship limit (429), a target projection or RPC deadline (502/503/504 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`) and an internal failure (500) to typed ApiError of catalog `cms.03c.related-content.v1`, commits no mutation on unresolved target authorization, and lets a target that later becomes private or unpublished drop out of the public projection while the rule history stays private. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Contract and error matrix, Normative application error catalog, Operation error coverage, Transaction and external seams
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-063** — CMS-03C-05 is consumed by the protected `CmsRelatedContentForm`, which builds `RelatedContentRuleRequest` from an ordered pin list with move controls (no drag-only path), an exclusion list, a derived rule chosen from the registry (`shared-terms`, `same-type-recent`) with `maxCandidates`, and the `eligibleCount` shown after save, states beside the lists that exclusions win, picks entries only from the caller-readable entry list (CMS-03B-13), and renders `related_target_ineligible` as one fixed message at the offending row without saying why; verification covers the pin and exclusion bounds, the uniform refusal, the 403/404 split and the focused result heading. [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6), Interaction Specification, Testing Obligations; [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-064** — CMS-03C-06 registers POST /api/v1/cms/governed-reviews with the strict `GovernedReviewSubmissionRequest` (`subjectKind`, `subjectId`, `subjectHash`) and returns the strict `GovernedReviewResource` as 201: `ResourceMeta` whose `contentHash` is the JCS SHA-256 of `{ subjectKind, subjectId, subjectHash, policyBasisHash, requiredDecisionCount, requiredCapabilities }`, plus `state` `open`, `subjectKind`, `subjectId`, `lineageId`, `subjectKey`, `subjectVersion`, `subjectHash`, `riskClass`, `requiredDecisionCount` 1-8, `recordedDecisionCount` 0-8, `requiredCapabilities` 1-16, `policyBasisHash`, `submittedAt`, `decidedAt`, `activatedAt` and `invalidatedReason`; generated OpenAPI and the discovered Hono route match the registry row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-065** — CMS-03C-06 accepts only a closed `subjectKind`, a `subjectId` that is the exact version row id in the caller's owner scope and a `subjectHash` of 64 lowercase hex equal to the stored `content_hash` (422 at `/subjectHash` otherwise), requires the subject state `draft` with no live review (else 409 `subject_not_submittable`) and, for a taxonomy version, a registered provider for every declared canonical domain (else 422 `canonical_source_unavailable` with `details.domain`), names the exact subject version by id and hash so no `If-Match` applies, and admits at most one live review per subject version through the partial unique index. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Contract and error matrix, Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-066** — CMS-03C-06 derives session and acting context server-side and serves only a holder of the subject kind's owner capability in the subject's owner party (`cms.template_designer` for a template or pattern version, `cms.taxonomy_curator` for a taxonomy version): a hidden or absent subject is 404, a visible subject without the capability is 403 `capability_missing`, `submitted_by` is server-derived, no step-up is required, and no person, party or authority identifier is serialized. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Per-operation authorization matrix, Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-067** — CMS-03C-06 requires an `Idempotency-Key` and no `If-Match`, enforces rate buckets of 30/min/user and 60/min/party, a 15,000 ms deadline, no-store and the Tier 2 p95 < 1,200 ms target, and in one transaction freezes the subject hash, the policy basis and the block digest, inserts the `open` review with its dependency rows, sets the subject to `review` and writes the audit record, emitting no event. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Transaction and external seams, Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Observability

- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-137** — CMS-03C-18 compares each NFC candidate (key, label or alias) with the domain's named read-only projection after normalization (NFKC, lowercase, trim, whitespace collapse) by exact equality only, never fuzzy-matches, accepts one domain and at most 64 candidates per call, and returns `source_unavailable` for a domain whose provider is `unbuilt`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Internal service operations
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-138** — CMS-03C-18 is executable only by its database-internal caller `cms_act_taxonomy_term` through `cms_term_overlap_check` (PUBLIC, anon, authenticated and every other role have EXECUTE revoked), reads only the domain's named read-only projection and never another shard's store directly (DEC-100), and returns only a canonical id and kind, never canonical content. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Permission, RLS and grants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-139** — CMS-03C-18 is read-only, bounded to 64 candidates and one domain per call, and applies the 2,000 ms provider timeout, so a call performs no insert, update, delete, audit row or outbox row and a retry is harmless. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Transaction and external seams
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-140** — CMS-03C-18 treats a provider fault or timeout as 503 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE` for the calling command with no editorial write, returns the typed `source_unavailable` outcome (never a `no_match`) for an unbuilt domain so that the caller can refuse `canonical_source_unavailable`, and never returns a success-shaped body on a fault. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Normative application error catalog
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-141** — CMS-03C-18 is consumed only by `cms_act_taxonomy_term` (create, rename and alias) through `cms_term_overlap_check`, never by the taxonomy-version activation preflight, which reads the domain registry; verification covers `match`, `no_match` and `source_unavailable` per domain, exact equality after normalization with no fuzzy match, the `role` provider reading the Shard 02 role facet assertion projection, the provider-fault 503 with no editorial write, and the absence of the RPC from browser routes and generated OpenAPI. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Testing Strategy; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-142** — CMS-03C-19 is exactly two database RPCs run by the Worker `scheduled` sweep as BE00 job type `cms.governed_activation.execute`: `platform_private.cms_claim_due_governed_activations(batch)` taking an integer 1-100 and returning at most `batch` strict `ClaimedGovernedActivation` records (`scheduleId`, `reviewId`, `subjectKind`, `subjectId`, `scheduleVersion`, `expectedVersion`, `leaseId`, `correlationId`), and `platform_private.cms_execute_governed_activation(schedule_id, expected_version, lease_id)` returning the strict `GovernedActivationExecutionResult` (`completed`, `blocked`, `failed_retryable` or `already_completed`, with `reasonCode`, `actualUtc` and `deviationSeconds`); neither is a browser route, proxy or client type. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6); [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-143** — CMS-03C-19 claims only due `pending` or `failed_retryable` schedules and executes only a claimed schedule whose `lease_id` still matches (a stale lease is refused with no effect), re-reading the review (`approved` at the schedule's stored `expected_version`), the subject (`scheduled` with an equal hash), the policy basis, the dependencies and the canonical providers, and rechecking that the schedule creator's designer or curator grant is unrevoked and has not ended before the fire instant. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6); [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Schedule execution (CMS-03B-20)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-144** — CMS-03C-19 grants EXECUTE only to the registered Worker `scheduled` sweep principal (PUBLIC, anon, authenticated and every other role revoked), re-derives the schedule creator's designer or curator authority at the fire instant by checking revocation and a grant end before the fire instant only, never rechecking MFA (DEC-120), and carries only identifiers, versions and correlation data in claims. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Per-operation authorization matrix
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-145** — CMS-03C-19 mirrors CMS-03B-20: the Worker `scheduled` sweep runs every minute with batch 25, claims under `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` with a version CAS from `pending` or `failed_retryable` to `executing` and a five-minute `lease_id` and `lease_until`, and applies the retry ladder in which a `failed_retryable` outcome sets `attempt_count` + 1 and `next_attempt_at` to now + 15 s, 60 s and 300 s for the first, second and third failure and a fourth consecutive retryable failure blocks the schedule with `retries_exhausted`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6); [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Internal service operations, Schedule execution (CMS-03B-20)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-146** — CMS-03C-19 returns an expired `executing` lease to `failed_retryable` with `attempt_count` + 1 before claiming, returns a repeated execution of a `completed` schedule as `already_completed` with no effect, blocks a terminal failure with the closed `reasonCode` (`approval_invalidated`, `dependency_changed`, `activation_authority_ended`, `canonical_source_unavailable`, `retries_exhausted`) making the subject `blocked` with the prior active version intact, cancels a schedule whose review is invalidated (`cancelled`, `approval_invalidated`) and returns the subject to `draft`, and runs a late schedule and records its `deviation_seconds`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Canonical records and fields, Operation error coverage
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-147** — CMS-03C-19 performs the `activate` switch for a due schedule, sets the schedule `completed` with `actual_at_utc` and `deviation_seconds`, and commits one activation event, the audit record and the schedule CAS atomically as CMS-03B-20 does; verification covers execution with only a revocation and grant-end recheck, worker lease expiry, the retry ladder and every `reasonCode`, a late run and a repeated run, and the absence of both RPCs from browser routes and generated OpenAPI. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Testing Strategy, State machine and concurrency; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-148** — CMS-03C-20 is the database RPC `platform_private.cms_resolve_locale_field(p_entry_id uuid, p_locale text, p_field_id uuid)` returning the strict `LocaleFieldResolution` (`fieldId`, `locale`, `mode` `none`, `localized` or `no_fallback`, `outcome` `resolved` or `missing`, `provenance` `authored`, `localized_fallback` or `missing`, `resolvedFromLocale`, `stale`, `blocking`, and `reasonCode` `no_fallback_missing`, `no_fallback_stale` or null) and the registered preflight provider `cms.locale.no_fallback_gate`; it is the one resolver used by the 03b `locale` preflight provider, the Shard 04 delivery projection and editorial reads, and it has no browser route. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-149** — CMS-03C-20 resolves a field by its `localizationMode`: with `none` the value is the source-locale value, and with `localized` (fallback permitted) it is the field in the target locale's own revision when present, otherwise the first locale of the active version's `fallbackChains[locale]` (ordered, at most 16, ending at the default locale) that has a value, recording `resolvedFromLocale` and provenance `localized_fallback`, otherwise `missing`; the `no_fallback` and stale clauses are owned by AC051 and AC052. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12); [BE03a](../../../wiki/specs/be/03a-content-schema-registry.md) §Locale configuration (OD-4)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-150** — CMS-03C-20 is executable only by the registered non-browser callers (the publication preflight `locale` provider, the Shard 04 delivery projection and the editorial reads; PUBLIC, anon, authenticated and every other role have EXECUTE revoked), returns identifiers, enums and booleans only and never locale text, and is absent from the browser route inventory and generated OpenAPI. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Permission, RLS and grants; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-151** — CMS-03C-20 is read-only and deterministic, performing no insert, update, delete, audit row or outbox row, so a retry is harmless and identical inputs resolve identically. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-152** — CMS-03C-20 is consumed by the 03b `locale` preflight provider, by the Shard 04 delivery projection (which adds no resolution rule of its own) and by `CmsLocaleVariantForm` per-field resolution, so a field blocked at publication is never resolved differently at delivery; verification covers the resolver for each `localizationMode`, the fallback chain walk and `resolvedFromLocale`, and the absence of the RPC from browser routes. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12), Security and abuse controls, Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-153** — CMS-03C-21 is the database RPC `platform_private.cms_related_content_public_targets(p_source_entry_id uuid, p_locale text, p_audience text)` returning an ordered array of at most 128 strict `RelatedPublicTarget` records (`entryId`, `source` `pin` or `derived`, a nullable `reasonCode` and `position` 0-127); it has no browser route and is absent from the browser route inventory. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas); [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-154** — CMS-03C-21 returns eligible pins first in `position` order (public eligibility: the target's lineage head is an `active` `publish` row for `(locale, audience)` and no hold applies), then derived candidates in rank order, always removes exclusions, and refills a vacated slot only from eligible derived candidates. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-155** — CMS-03C-21 is executable only by the registered non-browser callers (the Shard 04 delivery projection and the Slice 11 related-content recheck; PUBLIC, anon, authenticated and every other role have EXECUTE revoked), never returns a draft, hidden, unpublished or other-owner target, and never lets a recommendation grant access. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5), Security and abuse controls
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-156** — CMS-03C-21 is read-only, bounded to 128 targets, recomputed at every projection, preview and publication and never stored, performing no insert, update, delete, audit row or outbox row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-157** — CMS-03C-21 lets a target that becomes unpublished or unavailable disappear from the output while its editorial explanation and history stay private, so a stale or unavailable target is never exposed. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5), Operation error coverage
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-158** — CMS-03C-21 is consumed by the Shard 04 delivery projection (which re-implements neither resolution nor eligibility) and by the Slice 11 related-content recheck; verification covers pins-first ordering, the deterministic `shared-terms` and `same-type-recent` ranking and tie-breaks, the 128-target bound, refill only from eligible derived candidates, recomputation at every projection, and the absence of the RPC from browser routes. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Internal service operations, Testing Strategy, Cross-shard direction; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-159** — cmsTemplateContextRead registers GET /api/v1/cms/templates/context with an empty request and returns the strict `TemplateDesignerContext`: at most 64 owned active compiled content-type selectors (`id`, `typeKey`, `activeVersionId`, `activeVersion`, `sourceLocale`) and at most 128 owned registered, currently supported block selectors (`blockKey`, `blockVersion`), excluding owner IDs, grant records, renderer refs, manifests and policy internals; generated OpenAPI and the discovered Hono route match the registry row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Registry invariants, Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-160** — cmsTemplateContextRead accepts an empty request only: no request body, no CSRF requirement, no `Idempotency-Key`, no `If-Match` and no owner selector, with the owner and acting context derived server-side, and an invalid query is 400. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-161** — cmsTemplateContextRead serves only an authenticated human with confirmed acting-party membership and an active `cms.template_designer` grant, rechecks the actor, acting party, confirmed membership and current designer grant on every read in a private RPC that the browser and authenticated database roles cannot call or read through its source tables, answers a denied read as 403, and never reserves permission for a later write. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-162** — cmsTemplateContextRead is a no-store, read-only call that accepts neither `Idempotency-Key` nor `If-Match` and enforces read buckets of 60/min/user and 60/min/party, a 15,000 ms deadline and the Tier 2 target. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Route Registry
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-163** — cmsTemplateContextRead answers a denied read as 403, an invalid query as 400, a malformed provider projection as 502 and a dependency or deadline failure as 503 or 504 with a typed ApiError, and the POST independently rechecks all authority and compatibility so a stale context never authorizes a write. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-164** — cmsTemplateContextRead is consumed by the protected `CmsTemplateDesigner`, `CmsTemplateEditDesigner` and `CmsPatternVersionDesigner`, which load their picker options (active content types and supported blocks) from this same-origin no-store GET before enabling the form, disable submission with the corresponding sign-in, capability, rate-wait or retry state when the context is missing, denied or degraded, and neither extend the general auth-session resource nor grant designer authority in the browser; verification covers each denied or degraded state and that the POST revalidates authority. [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6), Interaction Specification, Testing Obligations; [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-165** — cmsTemplateLatestRead registers GET /api/v1/cms/templates/{templateKey} with `TemplateLatestQuery` (path) and returns the strict `TemplateVersionDetail` of the caller owner's latest version of the key: the `TemplateVersionResource` plus `slots`, `bindings`, `locale` and `audience`, with no owner or authority field; generated OpenAPI and the discovered Hono route match the registry row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Template reads, reserved keys and per-owner uniqueness (D7, E13, E14), Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-166** — cmsTemplateLatestRead accepts only a path `templateKey` that matches the template grammar and is not a reserved key (`context`, `versions`, `new`), resolves it only within the caller's owner scope, rejects a body, `Idempotency-Key` and `If-Match`, and answers a hidden or absent key with the same 404. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Route Registry, Template reads, reserved keys and per-owner uniqueness (D7, E13, E14)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-167** — cmsTemplateLatestRead serves only `cms.template_designer` in the caller's owner scope (a caller without the capability is 403), resolves the key only within that scope so that a key held by another owner is the same 404 as an unused key, and serializes no owner or authority field. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Template reads, reserved keys and per-owner uniqueness (D7, E13, E14), Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-168** — cmsTemplateLatestRead is a no-store, read-only call that accepts neither `Idempotency-Key` nor `If-Match`, carries the strong ETag `"{version}"` of the returned version, and enforces read buckets of 60/min/user and 60/min/party, a 15,000 ms deadline and the Tier 2 target. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Route Registry
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-169** — cmsTemplateLatestRead maps a malformed path (400 `INVALID_REQUEST`), a missing session (401), a missing capability (403 `TEMPLATE_FORBIDDEN`), a hidden or absent key (404 `TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND`), an unsupported body (415), the read limit (429), a dependency failure (502/503/504 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`) and an internal failure (500) to typed ApiError of catalog `cms.03c.template.v1`, and as a safe read is retried by a browser caller at most twice, after 250 ms and 750 ms (FE03), while server, queue and job callers retry it at most three times at 15 s, 60 s and 300 s with jitter (DEC-211). - [ ] **P2-S12-AC-170** — cmsTemplateLatestRead is consumed by the protected `CmsTemplateEditDesigner` at `/app/cms-content-modeling/templates/:templateKey`, which prefills the form from the owner's latest version, sends its `version` as `expectedVersion` to create a successor through CMS-03C-01, reconciles a 409 by refetching this read, reuses the idempotency key for an unchanged uncertain attempt, and never resolves `context`, `versions` or `new` as a template key; verification covers the prefill, the successor `expectedVersion`, the 409 reconciliation and the 403/404 split. [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6), Page and Route Definitions, Interaction Specification, Testing Obligations; [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-171** — cmsPatternLatestRead registers GET /api/v1/cms/patterns/{patternKey} with `PatternLatestQuery` (path) and returns the strict `PatternVersionDetail` of the caller owner's latest version of the key in any state: the `PatternVersionResource` plus the `blockTree` of 1-32 root nodes; generated OpenAPI and the discovered Hono route match the registry row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-172** — cmsPatternLatestRead accepts only a path `patternKey` that matches the pattern grammar and is not a reserved key (`context`, `versions`, `selector`, `new`), resolves it only within the caller's owner scope, rejects a body, `Idempotency-Key` and `If-Match`, and answers a hidden or absent key with the same 404. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Route Registry
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-173** — cmsPatternLatestRead serves only `cms.template_designer` in the caller's owner scope (a caller without the capability is 403), resolves the key only within that scope so that another owner's identical key is the same 404 as an unused key, and serializes no owner or authority field. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Per-operation authorization matrix, Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-174** — cmsPatternLatestRead is a no-store, read-only call that accepts neither `Idempotency-Key` nor `If-Match`, carries the strong ETag `"{version}"` of the returned version, and enforces read buckets of 60/min/user and 60/min/party, a 15,000 ms deadline and the Tier 2 target. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Route Registry
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-175** — cmsPatternLatestRead maps a malformed path (400), a missing session (401), a missing designer capability (403), a hidden or absent key (404), an unsupported body (415), the read limit (429), a read dependency or deadline (502/503/504 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`) and an internal failure (500) to typed ApiError of catalog `cms.03c.composition.v1`. - [ ] **P2-S12-AC-176** — cmsPatternLatestRead is consumed by the protected `CmsPatternVersionDesigner` and `CmsGovernedReviewPanel` at `/app/cms-content-modeling/patterns/:patternKey`, which address the owner's latest version by key, prefill the node list from it, send the read version as `expectedVersion` for a successor and show the version's lifecycle; verification covers the prefill, the successor `expectedVersion`, the 403/404 split and that the reserved keys never resolve as `:patternKey`.
