- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-177** — cmsTaxonomyVersionsRead registers GET /api/v1/cms/taxonomies/{taxonomyId}/versions with `TaxonomyVersionsQuery` (path) and returns the strict `TaxonomyVersionsPage` of at most 50 `TaxonomyVersionResource` items, newest first, and `pageVersion`; generated OpenAPI and the discovered Hono route match the registry row. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Route field validation matrix, Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-178** — cmsTaxonomyVersionsRead accepts only a UUID `taxonomyId` path (the vocabulary lineage id) with no query key (400 otherwise), rejects a body, `Idempotency-Key` and `If-Match`, and answers a hidden or absent vocabulary with the same 404. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Contract and error matrix
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-179** — cmsTaxonomyVersionsRead serves only `cms.taxonomy_curator` in the caller's owner scope (a caller without the capability is 403; a hidden or absent vocabulary is 404) over the owner's vocabulary lineage, and serializes no owner or authority field. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Per-operation authorization matrix, Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-180** — cmsTaxonomyVersionsRead is a no-store, read-only call that accepts neither `Idempotency-Key` nor `If-Match`, carries an ETag on the page version, and enforces read buckets of 60/min/user and 60/min/party, an 8,000 ms deadline and the Tier 1 p95 < 750 ms target. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Route Registry
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-181** — cmsTaxonomyVersionsRead maps a malformed path, query or cursor (400), a missing session (401), a missing curator capability (403 `TAXONOMY_FORBIDDEN`), a hidden or absent vocabulary (404 `TAXONOMY_NOT_FOUND`), an unsupported body if sent (415), a query-bound failure (422), the read limit (429), a read dependency or deadline (502/503/504 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`) and an internal failure (500) to typed ApiError of catalog `cms.03c.taxonomy.v1`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Contract and error matrix, Normative application error catalog
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-182** — cmsTaxonomyVersionsRead is consumed by the protected `CmsTaxonomyVersionDesigner` and `CmsGovernedReviewPanel` at `/app/cms-content-modeling/taxonomies/:taxonomyId`, which read the vocabulary's versions (newest first, at most 50) to show each version's lifecycle, review summary and permitted actions, while term actions render only against an `active` version and `taxonomy_not_active` links to the vocabulary's governed review; verification covers the 403/404 split, the 50-version bound and the active-version gating of term actions. [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6), Page and Route Definitions, Slice 12 definition governance states, Testing Obligations; [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-183** — cmsTaxonomyTermsRead registers GET /api/v1/cms/taxonomies/{taxonomyId}/terms with the strict `TaxonomyTermListQuery` and returns the strict `TaxonomyTermListPage` of at most 50 `TaxonomyTermListItem` rows (`termId`, `termKey`, `lifecycle`, `version`, `parentId`, `successorId`, at most 64 `labels` and at most 64 `aliases`), a `nextCursor` of at most 512 characters or null and `pageVersion`, ordered by the signed keyset `(termKey ASC, termId ASC)`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-184** — cmsTaxonomyTermsRead accepts only a UUID `taxonomyId` path, an optional `lifecycle` of `active`, `deprecated` or `merged`, a signed context-bound `cursor` of at most 512 characters and a `limit` of 1-50 (default 25), rejects every other key, a body and any mutation header, and refuses a response over 256 KiB as a 502 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE` response-contract failure. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Contract and error matrix
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-185** — cmsTaxonomyTermsRead serves only `cms.taxonomy_curator` in the caller's owner scope (a caller without the capability is 403; a hidden or absent vocabulary is 404), lists merged terms with their `successorId`, and serializes no owner or authority field. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Per-operation authorization matrix, Registry invariants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-186** — cmsTaxonomyTermsRead is a no-store, read-only call that accepts neither `Idempotency-Key` nor `If-Match`, carries an ETag on the page version and a signed cursor bound to the query and acting context, and enforces read buckets of 120/min/user and 240/min/party, an 8,000 ms deadline and the Tier 1 p95 < 750 ms target. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Route Registry
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-187** — cmsTaxonomyTermsRead answers a structurally malformed cursor with 400 and an expired, tampered or foreign-bound cursor with 409 (the client restarts from the first page), and maps session, capability, concealment, read-limit, read-dependency and internal failures to typed ApiError of catalog `cms.03c.taxonomy.v1` with no audit or outbox effect. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Contract and error matrix, Normative application error catalog, Operation error coverage
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-188** — cmsTaxonomyTermsRead is consumed by the protected `CmsTaxonomyTermForm`, which lists the vocabulary's terms with their lifecycle, keeps the term list cursor as URL state without serializing a protected value, shows a merged term's survivor redirect, renders `canonical_overlap` as "This matches a canonical {domain} record. Reference the canonical record instead" with the canonical id as copyable text and never creates the term, and links `taxonomy_not_active` to the vocabulary's governed review; verification covers term keyset order, cursor binding and merged terms listed with their successor. [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §§Slice 12 governed definition, composition, taxonomy, locale and related-content surfaces (DEC-113, DEC-114, DEC-135, DEC-137, DEC-138, D7, D9, D10, D11, OD-1, OD-2, OD-5, OD-6), Page and Route Definitions, Slice 12 definition governance states, Testing Obligations; [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-189** — Reserved keys (E13): `templateKey` must not equal `context`, `versions` or `new` and `patternKey` must not equal `context`, `versions`, `selector` or `new`, enforced in the contracts schema and in a SQL CHECK on `cms_template_versions.template_key` and `cms_pattern_versions.pattern_key`, so that a reserved key is 422 `TEMPLATE_VALIDATION_FAILED` (or `COMPOSITION_VALIDATION_FAILED`) at `/templateKey` or `/patternKey` with `details.reasonCode` `key_reserved` and can never resolve as the `{templateKey}` or `{patternKey}` of a latest read. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Registry invariants, Route field validation matrix, Canonical records and fields, Template reads, reserved keys and per-owner uniqueness (D7, E13, E14)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-190** — Per-owner uniqueness (E14): `UNIQUE (template_key, version)`, `UNIQUE (pattern_key, version)` and `UNIQUE (taxonomy_key, version)` are replaced by `UNIQUE (owner_id, <key>, version)` by forward migration, every lookup by key (the latest reads, parents and selectors) is scoped to the caller's owner party, and a key held by another owner is indistinguishable from an unused key, so that no cross-owner existence signal such as a 409 is possible. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Registry invariants, Canonical records and fields, Template reads, reserved keys and per-owner uniqueness (D7, E13, E14)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-191** — Lineage ids: `templateId`, `patternId` and `taxonomyId` are the stable lineage ids of (owner, key), the `id` of the version-1 row carried on every successor row as `template_id`, `pattern_id` and `taxonomy_id`; a version number is unique within its lineage, a successor names its parent through `supersedes_id`, and a partial unique index on each lineage id `WHERE state = 'active'` allows one active version per lineage. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Registry invariants, Canonical records and fields, Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-192** — Template audience (E4): `audience` follows the BE04c audience grammar `^[a-z0-9_-]{1,48}$`, matched as submitted and never trimmed, in the contracts schema and in a SQL CHECK on `cms_template_versions.audience`, so that an audience outside the grammar is refused 422 `TEMPLATE_VALIDATION_FAILED` with a bounded JSON-pointer violation at `/audience`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Canonical records and fields; [BE04c](../../../wiki/specs/be/04c-public-delivery-cache.md) §Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-193** — blockRegistryDigest: the server resolves every reachable block from the 03a safe registry and computes the lowercase SHA-256 of the RFC 8785/JCS array of the reachable, deduplicated `{blockKey,blockVersion,releaseDigest,propsSchemaHash,rendererRef,lifecycle}` tuples sorted by the UTF-8 bytes of `blockKey` and then numeric `blockVersion`; a client-supplied digest is only an equality expectation, compared with the server result and never stored in place of it, so the recomputed server digest is authoritative; and the server recomputes the digest at template create, template activate, pattern create, pattern activate and 03b publication preflight. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Block registry record and digest invariants, Route field validation matrix, Transaction and external seams, Security, performance, and recovery tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-194** — Block registry consumption: 03c consumes only the safe `BlockDefinitionRegistryRecord` projection (`id`, `version`, `blockKey`, `blockVersion`, `propsSchemaRef`, `propsSchemaHash`, `rendererRef`, `releaseDigest`, `lifecycle`, with the literal `resourceKind` `block_definition_registry_record` and no release principal, signature or raw manifest), never registers, updates or deletes a block, and before every template or pattern validation refetches the record or consumes the latest `cms.block.lifecycle.changed.v1`, so that a missing, withdrawn, incompatible, duplicate or unreachable block fails closed before mutation and a deprecated block follows the fixed compatibility policy. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Block registry record and digest invariants, Transaction and external seams, Event schemas
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-195** — Definition immutability: the definition fields of TemplateVersion, PatternVersion and TaxonomyVersion never change after creation (an edit is a successor version through CMS-03C-01, -12 or -16), activation makes the definition immutable, and the lifecycle columns `state` (`draft`, `review`, `approved`, `scheduled`, `active`, `superseded`, `retired`, `blocked`), `version`, `activated_at`, `retired_at` and `updated_at` advance only through the governed RPCs CMS-03C-06 to -09. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Registry invariants, Persisted model envelope, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-196** — Governed review tables: CMS-03C-06 to CMS-03C-11 serve the closed, code-owned `subject_kind` set (`template_version`, `pattern_version`, `taxonomy_version`) through one set of private tables keyed by `(subject_kind, subject_id)` (`cms_governed_reviews`, `cms_governed_review_decisions`, `cms_governed_review_assignments`, `cms_governed_review_dependencies` and, for scheduled activation, `cms_governed_activation_schedules`) so that slot semantics, MFA, assignment, strictest-policy counting and invalidation have one implementation; a later subject kind is added only by forward migration; and the 03a `cms_schema_reviews` set and the 03b editorial review tables keep their own tables and operations. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Registry invariants, Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Database Schema
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-197** — Subject state machine: `draft` becomes `review` at submission and `review` becomes `approved` when the last required approval lands; a rejection or invalidation returns the subject from `review` (or from `approved`, `scheduled` or `blocked`) to `draft` through an audited transition while the review stays `rejected` or `invalidated`; `approved` becomes `active` or `scheduled`; `scheduled` becomes `active` at execution or `blocked` when execution fails terminally; `blocked` becomes `active` or `scheduled` through a new activation command while its review is still `approved` and current; `active` becomes `superseded` in the transaction that activates a newer version of the lineage, or `retired`; `superseded` and `retired` are terminal; and the review states are `open` to `approved`, `rejected` or `invalidated` and `approved` to `invalidated`, with assignment create and revoke never changing the review state. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), State machine and concurrency
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-198** — Governed policy basis: the review count and slots are the strictest DEC-110 workflow policy among the content types the subject is bound to (a template or pattern version's `compatibleTypeIds`; for a taxonomy version the content types of the caller's owner scope whose keys appear in `allowedTypeKeys`), each resolved through the fail-closed `cms_editorial_workflow_policy_evidence` seam; the basis is the array of `{ contentTypeId, policyKey, policyVersion, policyHash }` sorted by `(policyKey, policyVersion, contentTypeId)`; `risk_class` is `protected` if any member is protected, `required_decision_count` is the largest member count and `required_capabilities` is the base slot `cms.reviewer` followed by each specialist capability in order of first appearance (at most 16); a subject with no resolvable bound type uses the registry member `editorial.default` version 1 (`CMS_GOVERNED_DEFAULT_POLICY`); and `policy_basis_hash` is the lowercase SHA-256 of the JCS basis array. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed policy basis, Canonical records and fields, Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-199** — Submitting a `template_version` requires the state `draft`, a compatibility-resolver success for the active version of every `compatibleTypeIds` member and a recomputed `blockRegistryDigest` equal to the stored one, and freezes the dependency kinds `block` (each reachable block record) and `content_type` (each compatible content type's active version id) into `cms_governed_review_dependencies`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-200** — Submitting a `pattern_version` requires the state `draft`, every nested pattern `active`, compatible and acyclic, and a recomputed digest equal to the stored one, and freezes the dependency kinds `block`, `pattern` (each nested pattern version) and `content_type`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-201** — Submitting a `taxonomy_version` requires the state `draft` and a registered provider for every declared canonical domain (else 422 `canonical_source_unavailable` with `details.domain`), and freezes the dependency kinds `content_type` (each content type named by `allowedTypeKeys`) and `canonical_domain` (the provider registry row of each declared domain). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-202** — Governed review invalidation producers: the consumers of `cms.block.lifecycle.changed.v1` (a withdrawn block), `cms.schema.activated.v1` (a changed bound policy or type version) and `cms.pattern.activated.v1` enqueue the idempotent job `cms.governed_review.recheck` (a typed platform-queue message, DEC-166; capability-grant revocation does not enqueue it and instead invalidates synchronously in the revoking transaction, AC-204, DEC-207), which selects live reviews through `cms_governed_review_dependencies (review_id, kind, ref_id)` of kind `block`, `pattern`, `content_type` or `canonical_domain`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Canonical records and fields, Permission, RLS and grants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-203** — Governed review recheck: the job `cms.governed_review.recheck` (`cms_recheck_governed_reviews(p_request jsonb)`, request `{ eventId, kind, refId, cursor, batch? }`) handles at most 500 reviews per run with a continuation cursor, is idempotent (an already `invalidated` review is skipped), rebuilds the hash, policy basis and identities of each live review and invalidates on any difference with the closed reason `dependency_changed` or `reviewer_authority_changed`, returning the subject to `draft` and, while the subject is `scheduled`, cancelling its schedule (`cancelled`, `approval_invalidated`) in the invalidating transaction; governed review state changes emit no event in Phase 2. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Permission, RLS and grants
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-204** — Reviewer authority invalidation: the closed reason `reviewer_authority_changed` is produced, under the 03b Review invalidation rules applied unchanged, when a counted approver's standing capability is revoked (the revoking transaction calls `cms_invalidate_governed_reviews_for_person(person_id, capability)`, a definer function not scoped to the revoker's party that invalidates, in that transaction, every `open` or `approved` review the person counted on under that capability, with the subject returning to `draft`) and when a counted approver's assignment is revoked while the review is `open`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Permission, RLS and grants; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Review invalidation
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-205** — Immediate activation: `activate` requires the subject `approved` or `blocked` (else 409 `subject_not_activatable`) and in one transaction makes it `active` (`version` + 1, `activated_at`), makes the lineage's previous `active` version `superseded` (`version` + 1), records `activated_at` on the review and advances its `version`, and commits the audit record and exactly one activation event; an activation that loses a race on the lineage is 409 `activation_conflict` and nothing commits. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-206** — Scheduled activation acceptance: `schedule` resolves `activateAt` through the 03b Time authority (E8: pinned tz database, DST rules, 60 s to 366 d horizon), runs the same kind-specific preflight, makes the subject `scheduled`, inserts one `pending` row in `cms_governed_activation_schedules` (a second live schedule for the subject is 409 `schedule_exists`), answers 202 and emits no event. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas); [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Time authority (E8)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-207** — Retirement: `retire` requires the subject `active` (else 409 `subject_not_retirable`) and needs no new approval or review decision and no dependency, policy, provider or preflight validity (DEC-190) while keeping the `reviewId` addressing, the review-`version` `If-Match`, the unconditional recent MFA and the subject kind's owner capability, makes it `retired`, emits the same event type naming the retired version, and a retired version fails the 03b `template` preflight and the 03a compatibility resolver as `WITHDRAWN`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-208** — Activating or scheduling a `template_version` runs the compatibility resolver for every `compatibleTypeIds` member and recomputes the block digest before the switch. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-209** — Activating or scheduling a `pattern_version` checks the state of every nested pattern and recomputes the block digest before the switch. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-210** — Activating or scheduling a `taxonomy_version` requires a registered provider for every declared canonical domain, else 422 `canonical_source_unavailable` with `details.domain`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1), Contract and error matrix
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-211** — Pattern graph: a pattern tree is a bounded forest of depth at most 8 and at most 512 nodes after expanding nested pattern references, with unique sibling node `key`s, no cycle through a nested lineage (the visited set is the lineage `pattern_id`) and every nested pattern `active` and listing every `compatibleTypeIds` member of the new pattern; a violation is typed (422 `pattern_depth_exceeded`, `pattern_nodes_exceeded` and `nested_pattern_not_active`; 409 `pattern_cycle`), is refused before save and again before publish, and `node_count` stores the expanded count. For entry publication, the live nested-pin active/compatibility recheck is the pattern provider's `nested_pattern_not_active` failed reason (DEC-199), not a new transport status; nonnull canonical detached snapshots do not reopen provenance pins. Every block node of an expanded tree also satisfies its pinned block version's `allowedChildren` and `slotRules` (DEC-210): a violation at CMS-03C-12 save, CMS-03C-02 placement or CMS-03C-15 accept or detach is 422 `COMPOSITION_VALIDATION_FAILED` with `details.reasonCode` `block_rules_violated` and nothing written, and at publication the pattern provider fails `pattern_block_rules_violated`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Security and abuse controls, Normative application error catalog
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-212** — Linked-update scan: when a pattern version is activated, the idempotent consumer job `cms.pattern.linked_update_scan` (a typed platform-queue message with payload `{ patternId, patternVersionId }`, DEC-166) appends a `pending_diff` row, pinned to the unchanged version and carrying `pending_pattern_version`, for each `linked` instance on a draft revision whose pinned version is older and whose entry type is compatible with the new version, and leaves instances on reviewed or published revisions on their pin as history. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Canonical records and fields, Event schemas
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-213** — Linked-update scan bounds: `cms.pattern.linked_update_scan` processes at most 500 instances per run with a continuation cursor and is idempotent, so a re-delivered `cms.pattern.activated.v1` appends no duplicate `pending_diff` row and a long instance list continues from the cursor. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-214** — Pattern update `accept`: for each listed instance a listed path that is not a collision adopts the target value, a local override on a path the target did not change is retained, every collision takes the explicit `yours` or `theirs` choice (on a `props` collision `theirs` takes the target `props` and `yours` keeps the override; on a `structure` collision `theirs` adopts the target's block identity and `children` there, keeping that path's override only when the target left its `props` unchanged), and the command appends an `active` row pinned to the target version whose kept overrides each name a node of the target tree; on a `removed_root` collision `theirs` appends a `retired` row, and `yours` on a `structure` or `removed_root` collision appends a `detached` row materializing the tree every explicit choice resolves to (an ordinary `detach` copies the current effective content unchanged instead), so no choice is discarded and no row is ever left linked and pinned to a version whose forest lacks its block root; a complete answer set that cannot form one consistent reachable tree is refused atomically 422 `COMPOSITION_VALIDATION_FAILED`; accept never creates a row for a block root the target adds (DEC-193). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Route field validation matrix
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-215** — Pattern update `detach`: the command appends a `detached` row that materializes the instance's current effective content (the full subtree of its block root in the pinned version, nested pattern references expanded in place, every override applied) as a detached local block tree in `detached_tree`, bounded by the 512-node pattern limit with no key-count refusal, keeps the pin of the row it follows as provenance only and stops the instance receiving updates; it is admitted for any linked pattern-origin row, including a `retired` one, and the render plan, restore and the revision-append carry-over resolve the tree (DEC-193). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Per-operation authorization matrix
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-216** — Pattern insertion target: CMS-03C-02 inserts only on the entry's current draft revision (effective state `draft` per E2, else 409 `revision_not_editable`) that the caller can read (patterns apply to entry revisions only, DEC-176), and `patternId` is a lineage id of the caller's owner scope. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-217** — Pattern insertion version: the named pattern version must be `active` and its `compatibleTypeIds` must list the revision's content type, else 422 `pattern_version_not_active` or `pattern_incompatible`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-218** — Pattern insertion rows (DEC-175, DEC-181, DEC-185): the request `slotPath` must exist in the revision's template (or be a root path when the entry has no template); a top-level nested pattern reference (`kind: pattern`) in the pinned forest is expanded at placement into that pattern's own block roots, recursively, so every row carries a block; one placement writes one append-only row per block root of the pinned pattern version's forest after that expansion, one or more rows (at most 512, the node cap after expansion) in one transaction and all-or-nothing, at consecutive `position` values in forest order starting one above the greatest `position` of the revision's slot (0 for an empty slot) under the revision/slot lock (DEC-185), and answers 201 `PatternPlacementResource`, an `items` list of one or more `CompositionInstanceResource` in position order; each row stays pinned to the placed `(patternId, patternVersion)`, carries its block root's block identity in `block_key` and `block_version` with that root's `key` as the suffix of its `path`, keeps the nodes below a block root inside the pinned tree and stores the server-recomputed digest, the link mode and the `overrides` that lie under its root; `overrides` keys are normalized node paths under the inserted root (`/{nodeKey}(/{nodeKey})*`, at most 512 characters) that each name a node of the pinned tree, any other key being 422 `COMPOSITION_VALIDATION_FAILED` at `/overrides`; `(revision_id, path)` is unique per version, and an override collision produces `pending_diff` and never overwrites; an optional `roots` places only the named block roots (how the author places the block roots a newer version adds, DEC-193), a path whose newest row is not `retired` is occupied (409 `COMPOSITION_VERSION_CONFLICT`, nothing written), and a path whose newest row is `retired` is free, the placed row being that path's next version at its kept position (DEC-185, DEC-193). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Route field validation matrix, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas), Transaction and external seams, Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-219** — Composition write effect: every composition write (CMS-03C-02, which appends one row per block root (DEC-181), CMS-03C-15, the linked-update scan and the revision-append carry-over) appends a row, never changes the revision's `contentHash` (composition instances are not part of it) and invalidates the revision's live editorial review in the same transaction, with the reason `dependency_changed` for CMS-03C-02 because the manifest `blocks` and `patterns` groups changed. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-220** — Vocabulary model: a vocabulary is identified by its stable lineage id `taxonomyId`, a TaxonomyVersion is the reviewed rule set (shape, allowlists and declared canonical overlap domains), and the terms belong to the vocabulary, so `cms_terms` carries `taxonomy_id` and `UNIQUE (taxonomy_id, term_key, version)` replaces `UNIQUE (taxonomy_version_id, term_key, version)`, a successor version never duplicates or re-keys a term, and term ids stay stable across versions and merges. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-221** — Term action preconditions: CMS-03C-03 runs under the vocabulary's `active` TaxonomyVersion (no active version is 409 `taxonomy_not_active`), `parentId` other than null requires shape `hierarchical`, and assigning a term to a field requires the vocabulary's active version to list the entry content type's key in `allowedTypeKeys` and the field key in `allowedFieldKeys`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Transaction and external seams
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-222** — Canonical overlap (DEC-135): the overlap check runs only against the `canonicalOverlapDomains` declared by the vocabulary's active version, so for `create`, `rename` and `alias` over a declared built domain a `match` from CMS-03C-18 refuses the action with 422 `TAXONOMY_VALIDATION_FAILED` `canonical_overlap` carrying `details.domain` and `details.canonicalId` and creates nothing, `merge` and `deprecate` run no overlap check, and a vocabulary that declares no domain is never checked. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Transaction and external seams, Normative application error catalog
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-223** — Unbuilt canonical domain (DEC-135): a declared domain whose provider is `unbuilt` is refused with the typed `canonical_source_unavailable` carrying `details.domain` at taxonomy-version activation and, defensively, at every `create`, `rename` and `alias` action, and a provider fault or timeout is 503 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE` with no editorial write. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Internal service operations
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-224** — Term actions: `create` inserts a term with lifecycle `active` and its first labels, `rename` appends a new label version and never changes the key, `alias` appends an alias, `deprecate` moves the term to `deprecated` (still resolvable, not newly assignable), a `merged` term never reactivates, and label versions are immutable (a replacement creates a new version). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Canonical records and fields, Security and abuse controls
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-225** — Term merge: `merge` locks the survivor and the retired term (`FOR UPDATE` in id order), requires a survivor that is `active` or `deprecated` and a retired term that is not already `merged`, sets the retired term `merged` with its `successor_id`, and keeps the retired id as a permanent redirect. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Security and abuse controls
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-226** — Term merge convergence: `merge` re-points every `active` TermAssignment of the retired term to the survivor by appending superseding assignment versions, converging up to 5,000 assignments in the same transaction and continuing a larger set through the idempotent job `cms.taxonomy.merge_converge` (a BE00 jobs row bound to the merge command's actor and acting party) from a durable cursor, with the retired id resolving to the survivor throughout. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Transaction and external seams
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-227** — Related-content targets (D11): a pin target must exist, differ from the source, have lifecycle `active`, belong to the same owner party as the source and be readable by the caller under the draft-read predicate, an exclusion target must exist and be readable by the caller (its lifecycle is not constrained), and an ineligible, hidden or absent pin or exclusion is one uniform 422 `RELATED_CONTENT_VALIDATION_FAILED` `related_target_ineligible` at `/pins/{n}` or `/exclusions/{n}` with identical bodies, so that a write is never an existence oracle. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5), Route field validation matrix, Security and abuse controls, Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-228** — Related-content rule storage: rules are append-only versions, a request replaces the whole rule set (pins in array order as `position`, exclusions and the derived rule) by appending `revoked` rows for removed items and `active` rows for new ones, the strong `If-Match` is the source entry `version`, and derived rows carry no target while pin and exclude rows require one. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5), Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-229** — Derived-rule registry (OD-5): the code-owned registry `CMS_RELATED_RULES` has `shared-terms` (reason `shared_terms`) and `same-type-recent` (reason `same_type_recent`) at version 1, a rule row selects exactly one, `derivedRule.reasonCode` is an equality expectation of the registry value, `maxCandidates` is 1-128, the rule key and version are validated by `platform_private.cms_related_rule_valid`, and an unknown key or version is 422 `derived_rule_unknown`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5), Route field validation matrix, Canonical records and fields
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-230** — Related-content `eligibleCount`: a rule is never evaluated at write time except to compute `eligibleCount` (the eligible pins plus the derived candidates for the source revision's locale and the audience of the source's most recent active publication, with 0 derived when the source has none), and the count never exceeds 128. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5), Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-231** — Derived `shared-terms` ranking: candidates are entries of the same owner with an `active` publication head for the requested locale and audience, other than the source, not excluded and not already pinned, ranked by the number of active terms shared with the source's term assignments (the published revision's in a public projection, the draft's in an editorial preview) descending, then the target head's `created_at` descending, then `entryId` ascending. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-232** — Derived `same-type-recent` ranking: the same candidate set restricted to the source's content type is ranked by the target head's `created_at` descending then `entryId` ascending, and at most `maxCandidates` derived candidates are used. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Related-content eligibility and derived rules (D11, OD-5)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-233** — Locale configuration check (OD-4): CMS-03C-04 requires `locale` to be a member of the active content-type version's `supportedLocales` (422 `LOCALE_VALIDATION_FAILED` otherwise) and `fallbackChain` to equal, element for element, that version's `fallbackChains` entry for `locale` (`[]` exactly when `locale` is its `defaultLocale`); any difference in membership, order or length is 409 `LOCALE_VERSION_CONFLICT` with `details.reasonCode` `FALLBACK_CHAIN_MISMATCH` and `details.activeFallbackChain` (at most 16 tags) and writes no variant, and after a successor changes the chain the new chain applies and the old chain is refused. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Normative application error catalog, Transaction and external seams, Testing Strategy; [BE03a](../../../wiki/specs/be/03a-content-schema-registry.md) §Locale configuration (OD-4)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-234** — Derived locale variant state: a stored LocaleVariant row is `draft` (written by CMS-03C-04) or `stale` (appended by the source-change fan-out), while the browser `LocaleVariantState` also shows `review` and `approved`, derived from the editorial review of the locale revision by the 03b effective-state rule (`submitted` shows `review`; `approved` and `published` show `approved`), and `untranslated` when no variant exists for the locale. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12), Canonical records and fields, State machine and concurrency
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-235** — Per-field staleness: the variant records `field_source_hashes` (the source value hash of each translated field at write time), a field is stale when the current source value hash differs, `staleFieldIds` carries those fields, unchanged fields keep their approved state, and explicit revalidation is a new CMS-03C-04 write with the current `sourceHash` that appends a `draft` row and returns to `approved` only through the revision's editorial review. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12), Canonical records and fields, Transaction and external seams
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-236** — Locale fan-out bound (D12): a source-locale change appends stale rows synchronously for at most 32 dependent locale variants per entry, and a request that would create a 33rd distinct locale variant is refused 409 `LOCALE_VERSION_CONFLICT` `locale_limit_reached`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-237** — Locale event identity (D12): every locale variant and every stale row is a version of the locale aggregate `(entry, locale)`, and the outbox identity of `cms.localization.changed.v1` is aggregateType `cms_locale_variant`, aggregateId a UUIDv5 over the entry id and locale under the fixed namespace `cms.locale.aggregate.v1`, and aggregateVersion the variant row's `version`, so that the author RPC and the fan-out writer never share a dedupe key. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route field validation matrix, Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12), Event schemas
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-238** — Preflight provider `cms.pattern.active_digest` version 1 (category `pattern`, `database`) is registered by forward migration as a newer registry row replacing `preflight.reference_gate`. Live pinned PatternVersions require active state, equal frozen content hashes and equal recomputed reachable block-registry digests; every reached pinned nested version must also remain active and satisfy the existing graph compatibility rule (DEC-199). Failure reasons are `pattern_not_active`, `nested_pattern_not_active`, `pattern_digest_changed` and `pattern_block_rules_violated` (DEC-210). A nonnull canonical detached tree validates its own blocks/digest without reopening its retained pattern provenance; NULL-tree linked/legacy detached rows keep live checks, and mixed live/provenance use still validates the live use. Manifest provenance identities are retained (DEC-193). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Preflight providers registered by Slice 12; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Publication preflight registry (D19, DEC-134, D25)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-239** — Preflight provider `cms.taxonomy.assignment_integrity` version 1 (category `taxonomy`, `database`) is registered by forward migration as a newer registry row replacing `preflight.reference_gate`, requires every `active` TermAssignment of the revision to name an `active` term (a `deprecated` term is tolerated for an existing assignment and a `merged` term whose assignment has not converged fails `term_merged_unconverged`), the vocabulary's active version to still list the entry type key and field key (`allowlist_changed`), and every manifest taxonomy version id to be `active` (`taxonomy_version_not_active`). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Preflight providers registered by Slice 12; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Publication preflight registry (D19, DEC-134, D25)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-240** — Preflight provider `cms.locale.no_fallback_gate` version 1 (category `locale`, `database`) is registered by forward migration as a newer registry row replacing `preflight.reference_gate` and evaluates every `no_fallback` field of the revision's schema for the revision's locale in one statement: every blocking resolution fails the category with `no_fallback_missing` or `no_fallback_stale`, in the `schedule`, `publish` and `execute` phases a revision whose locale variant is not in effective state `approved`, `scheduled` or `published` fails with `locale_variant_not_approved` (never at `submit`; a missing value outranks a stale one, which outranks the approval check; DEC-192), and otherwise the category passes; the `route`, `media` and `privacy` categories stay on the reference gate. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12), Preflight providers registered by Slice 12; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Publication preflight registry (D19, DEC-134, D25)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-241** — Preflight dispatch: each Slice 12 provider is implemented in the deployed preflight evaluation before its registry row is inserted, so that no category evaluates `unavailable` with `provider_unavailable` because its provider is not implemented, and the contracts' per-category closed reason sets and the fixed reason copy accept exactly the reasons of `cms.pattern.active_digest` (including `pattern_not_active`, `nested_pattern_not_active` and `pattern_digest_changed`, DEC-199), `cms.taxonomy.assignment_integrity` and `cms.locale.no_fallback_gate`. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Preflight providers registered by Slice 12; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Publication preflight registry (D19, DEC-134, D25)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-242** — Taxonomy-version resolution (DEC-141): `platform_private.cms_resolve_taxonomy_version(p_taxonomy_version_id uuid)` resolves any taxonomy version id of the caller's owner scope, in any state from `draft` through `retired`, to `{ taxonomyId, taxonomyKey, taxonomyVersion, state, contentHash }` and answers an absent or out-of-scope id with the non-disclosing `comparison_unavailable`, so that CMS-03B-03 comparison and the CMS-03B-04 lineage compare recorded taxonomy versions by that identity and no longer fail closed for a non-empty reference. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Receiving obligations from Slice 10 (DEC-141), Testing Strategy; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Recorded taxonomy and composition references in comparison and restore (DEC-141, D-3, D-4)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-243** — Restore of composition instances (DEC-141, DEC-201): `cms_restore_revision` selects the named source revision's newest row at each path before eligibility and carries only its `active` rows into the new draft revision. A copied child's recorded parent is resolved within that source owner/revision by logical path to the newest eligible active copied parent, including historical parent references; an excluded parent or collapsed cycle refuses the entire restore atomically, without resurrecting history, dropping/detaching a child, fabricating topology or retaining a cross-revision edge. Fresh parent-first copies have immediate `carried_from_id` provenance, keep path, position, link mode and detached local block tree (DEC-193), and are re-pinned to the current registry, so that a pinned block version, or a block of a detached tree, that is `withdrawn` fails the restore 409 `migration_chain_incomplete`, a pinned pattern version that is no longer `active` fails it the same way unless the instance is `detached`, and a slot path absent from the current compatible template fails it 409 `template_incompatible`, with nothing fabricated. Pending/draft heads are not carried. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Receiving obligations from Slice 10 (DEC-141), Testing Strategy; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Recorded taxonomy and composition references in comparison and restore (DEC-141, D-3, D-4)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-244** — Restore of term assignments (DEC-141): each `active` TermAssignment of the source revision is carried to the new revision, an assignment to an `active` or `deprecated` term carrying unchanged, a `merged` term re-pointed to its surviving successor, and an unknown or other-vocabulary term failing the restore 409 `migration_chain_incomplete`, and the new revision's `taxonomy_version_ids` are the vocabularies' current `active` version ids. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Receiving obligations from Slice 10 (DEC-141), Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-245** — Revision append carry-over (DEC-141, DEC-198, DEC-200): `cms_create_revision` (from its base revision) and `cms_resolve_conflict` (from the current draft `theirs`, never the conflict base, DEC-209) select newest-per-path source rows before eligibility and carry both `active` and `pending_diff` compositions as fresh append-only rows with immediate `carried_from_id` provenance, parent remapping by the owner/revision-bound source parent path to its newest eligible copied head (DEC-200), with atomic whole-command refusal if no eligible parent remains or the remapped graph is unrepresentable, unchanged position, bindings, overrides and full detached local tree (DEC-193). Pending state, existing pin and `pending_pattern_version` survive unchanged; autosave neither drops visible content nor accepts/clears the unanswered update or resurrects older active history. Explicit command pointers retain their defined exception. Restore separately translates the named source's eligible `active` rows under AC243, not the current draft or this pending-row expansion. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Receiving obligations from Slice 10 (DEC-141), Canonical records and fields, Testing Strategy
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-246** — Taxonomy field values (DEC-141): once Slice 12 registers the taxonomy authority, the 03b `taxonomy` value encoder replaces its fail-closed `taxonomy_source_unavailable` with the rule that each `termId` must be an `active` term of a vocabulary whose active version lists the entry's content type key and the field's key, and a merged, deprecated-for-assignment, unknown or other-vocabulary term is the uniform 422 `VALIDATION_FAILED` reason `term_unavailable`, with the revision's `taxonomy_version_ids` being the active version ids of the vocabularies of its terms. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Receiving obligations from Slice 10 (DEC-141), Testing Strategy; [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Value encodings by field kind
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-247** — Render plan (DEC-165 receiving obligation, DEC-185): `platform_private.cms_revision_render_plan` takes, for each `(revision_id, path)`, the current (highest `version`) composition instance in state `draft`, `active` or `pending_diff` in template slot order, then `position` (so the rows of one placement render in forest order and two placements in one slot stay grouped), then path (bytewise) and id, renders a `pending_diff` instance from its pinned version, renders a row that carries a nonnull `detached_tree` from that tree, never from its provenance pin or overrides, while a plain `detached` row with a NULL `detached_tree` keeps its defined pinned render and checker behavior (DEC-193), reads each block-root row of a pattern placement as one block root at its own `path` (DEC-175, DEC-181), followed by the descendant blocks of its effective tree at `/composition/{rowId}/nodes/{i}` in canonical preorder (DEC-214), and excludes `superseded` and `retired` rows, so that the accessibility checker input and the projection compiler read one derivation of what readers get, and field placement changes only if a field-to-slot binding is introduced (none is: template `bindings` map named keys to domain projections). [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Pattern versions and linked updates (D9, OD-2), Canonical records and fields, Request/Response Contracts (Zod 4 schemas); [BE05c](../../../wiki/specs/be/05c-portability-quality-lifecycle.md) §Accessibility and content-quality checker
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-248** — Event relay (DEC-166 receiving obligation): Slice 12's forward migration makes every Slice 12 event claimable by the outbox relay under the exact aggregate type its producer writes, including `cms_locale_variant` for `cms.localization.changed.v1` and the aggregate types of `cms.template.activated.v1`, `cms.pattern.activated.v1` and `cms.taxonomy.changed.v1` (`cms_template_version`, `cms_pattern_version`, `cms_taxonomy_version` and `cms_term`, DEC-180 G3), and registers the `cms.pattern.` and `cms.taxonomy.` producer prefixes only as insert-if-absent, so that no claimed event has an unregistered producer or an unlisted aggregate type. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Event schemas, Locale field resolution (DEC-121, DEC-138) and fan-out (D12)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-249** — Activation events: `cms.template.activated.v1` `{ templateId, templateVersionId }` and `cms.pattern.activated.v1` `{ patternId, patternVersionId }` (a new event type registered by Slice 12) are emitted exactly once by CMS-03C-09 only on a committed immediate activation, a committed retirement (naming the retired version) or a committed scheduled execution, in the transaction with the switch and the audit record, have the outbox identity of the switched subject version row (aggregateType `cms_template_version` or `cms_pattern_version`, aggregateId the activated or retired version id, aggregateVersion that row's `lifecycle_version` after the switch, DEC-188 (correcting DEC-180 G3)) so that every committed command has a unique triple, carry identifiers only (no content values, labels, locale text, target names, secrets, tokens or authority), and are not emitted for a schedule acceptance, a refusal or a governed review state change. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Event schemas, Governed activation, retirement and scheduled activation (DEC-113, OD-6), Governed review machinery (DEC-137, DEC-113, OD-1)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-250** — Taxonomy changed event: `cms.taxonomy.changed.v1` `{ taxonomyId, taxonomyVersionId }` is emitted once per committed CMS-03C-03 term action (carrying the vocabulary's active version id) and by CMS-03C-09 after a taxonomy-version activation or retirement, as an identifier-only event whose outbox identity is the term row for a term action (aggregateType `cms_term`, aggregateId the term id, aggregateVersion the term `version` after the action) and the subject version row for an activation or retirement (aggregateType `cms_taxonomy_version`, aggregateId the version id, aggregateVersion that row's `lifecycle_version` after the switch, DEC-188 (correcting DEC-180 G3)), and whose consumers refetch aliases and terms under their own authority. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Event schemas, Taxonomy versions, canonical projection and term governance (D10, OD-1, DEC-135), Route Registry
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-251** — Persistence `cms_governed_reviews`: rows are inserted only through `cms_submit_governed_review`, with `state` one of `open`, `approved`, `rejected`, `invalidated`, `subject_kind` one of the three subject kinds, a polymorphic `subject_id` validated by `platform_private.cms_governed_subject_resolve(kind,id)` in the RPC and a BEFORE INSERT trigger, `subject_hash`, `policy_basis_hash` and `approval_evidence_hash` as 64 lowercase hex (the last the JCS SHA-256 of `{ policyBasisHash, subjectHash, subjectId, subjectKind }`), `required_decision_count` 1 to 8 with `recorded_decision_count` at most that count, `required_capabilities` of 1 to 16 and `policy_basis` of 1 to 64 entries, CHECKs tying `protected` to at least 2 decisions and 2 capabilities, `invalidated` to `invalidated_reason` and `approved` or `rejected` to `decided_at`, and `UNIQUE(subject_kind, subject_id) WHERE state IN ('open','approved')`; the frozen columns are immutable, only the named RPCs advance `state`, the recorded count, `activated_at`, `version` and `updated_at` under CAS, and direct UPDATE, DELETE and table grants are revoked. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Canonical records and fields, Persisted model envelope
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-252** — Persistence `cms_governed_review_decisions`: rows are append-only (`state` `recorded`, `version` 1, `updated_at = created_at`, UPDATE, DELETE and direct grants revoked) with `UNIQUE(review_id, reviewer_person_id)`, a NOT NULL `step_up_at`, the `assignment_id` and `assignment_version` recorded, `capability` the satisfied slot, `decision` `approve` or `reject`, a `reason` of 1 to 2000 code points (`char_length`) with `comment_hash` and `reviewed_hash` as 64 hex, the reviewer, slot capability and MFA instant server-derived, `reason` readable only by its decider, and a BEFORE INSERT trigger forbidding a `reviewer_person_id` equal to the review's `submitted_by` or the subject version's creator. - [ ] **P2-S12-AC-271** — Definition governance states: for `governedReviewState` (CMS-03C-10 and -11), `selectorState` and `latestReadState` (CMS-03C-13 and -17 and the template, pattern, vocabulary-version and term reads), `diffState` (CMS-03C-14) and `commandState` (CMS-03C-01 to -09, -12, -15 and -16) the island renders `idle`, `loading` (a skeleton after 250 ms and only the commit control disabled), `success`, `empty`, `error`, `degraded` and `disabled` exactly as the state table states, server-authoritative, no-store and never optimistic: a 401 `STEP_UP_REQUIRED` routes to `/step-up?returnTo=`, a 403 `capability_missing` or `separation_of_duties` renders the `CapabilityGate` copy, 409 reasons render fixed copy and refetch, 422 reasons render fixed copy at the field, an unknown mutation outcome renders pending and reconciles by refetching the review, subject or entry before any retry, and a degraded diff is never kept but re-required. - [ ] **P2-S12-AC-272** — Governed surface privacy: decision reasons, the policy basis, pattern diff values, canonical ids and every assignment identifier are re-derived by the server and never enter a public or private cache, an analytics event, a Realtime body, a URL or a log, and a browser never calls CMS-03C-18 to CMS-03C-21 because no browser route, form, proxy or client type exists for them. - [ ] **P2-S12-AC-273** — End-to-end journeys: Playwright, using the real routes and two people, creates, submits, assigns, decides (ordinary and protected) and activates, schedules and retires a template, a pattern and a vocabulary; shows a dependency invalidation returning a subject to draft, a linked pattern update accepted and detached, a term merged with convergence, a locale variant with a `no_fallback` block, and related content pinned and excluded; and runs keyboard-only and at 200% zoom with no diff value, canonical id or person id in the URL, storage, logs or telemetry. - [ ] **P2-S12-AC-274** — Typed refusals: within the Slice 12 catalogs a refusal carries exactly one lowercase `details.reasonCode` from the closed list (`capability_missing`, `separation_of_duties`, `subject_not_submittable`, `review_not_open`, `duplicate_decision`, `specialist_slot_unsatisfiable`, `reviewer_not_eligible`, `assignment_exists`, `assignment_limit`, `dependency_changed`, `subject_not_activatable`, `subject_not_retirable`, `activation_conflict`, `schedule_exists`, `revision_not_editable`, `pattern_cycle`, `diff_has_collisions`, `taxonomy_not_active`, `locale_limit_reached`, `FALLBACK_CHAIN_MISMATCH`, `key_reserved`, `pattern_version_not_active`, `pattern_incompatible`, `nested_pattern_not_active`, `pattern_depth_exceeded`, `pattern_nodes_exceeded`, `diff_too_large`, `canonical_overlap`, `canonical_source_unavailable`, `term_unavailable`, `derived_rule_unknown`, `related_target_ineligible`, `block_rules_violated` (DEC-210) and the 03b time tokens, each keeping the single status the catalog lists it under: `pattern_version_not_active`, `pattern_incompatible` and `nested_pattern_not_active` are 422 and every governed 409 is `GOVERNED_REVIEW_VERSION_CONFLICT`) plus only the members named for it, a provider fault or provider-reported timeout is 503, an invalid provider response 502 and the route deadline 504 `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE` and never a success-shaped body, and an unmapped status or a message not generated by its operation catalog is a contract failure treated as 500 `INTERNAL_ERROR` without leaking the original payload. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Normative application error catalog
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-275** — Registry parity: CI matches the discovered Hono routes and the generated OpenAPI to every Slice 12 browser operation ID (CMS-03C-01 to CMS-03C-17 and the five supporting reads) on method and path, request, success and error schemas, auth, CORS, rate, timeout, cache, idempotency, ETag and SLO, and CMS-03C-18 to CMS-03C-21 are absent from the browser route inventory and generated OpenAPI. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §§Route Registry, Internal service operations, Contract and route tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-276** — Lock order: the Slice 12 writers (CMS-03C-01 to CMS-03C-09, CMS-03C-12, CMS-03C-15, CMS-03C-16 and CMS-03C-19, the linked-update scan, the revision-append carry-over, the recheck job and the term merge) take their locks in the Slice 12 lock order, the BE03b global write-path order extended by DEC-157 with one position for term rows, and never acquire an earlier position after a later one (the ten CMS-03B-06 decision steps order checks, not locks), re-prove their capability under the locks, and map `deadlock_detected` and a lock-acquisition failure to the 409 code of the operation's catalog (`*_VERSION_CONFLICT`) with nothing committed and the same `Idempotency-Key` reusable. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Slice 12 lock order (DEC-157, DEC-180); [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §Write-path lock order and authority fencing (H1-H3, lane H round 2)
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-277** — Concurrency scenarios: template activation versus block withdrawal, linked pattern update versus local override, term merge versus assignment, locale source edit versus translation, related target unpublish, duplicate and out-of-order events, worker lease expiry and restore-epoch fencing each have a deterministic tested outcome. [BE03c](../../../wiki/specs/be/03c-composition-taxonomy-localization.md) §Authorization, persistence, and concurrency tests
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-278** — No leakage: no private locale text, draft composition, unauthorized target, capability or canonical-domain authority enters a public projection, an event payload, a log or a cache. - [ ] **P2-S12-AC-287** — CMS-03B-10 initial revision preserves SQL NULL when the exact active content-type version has no default; a nonempty bindings list, another compatible template, or a newer template version must not supply a fallback. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-288** — CMS-03B-10 initial revision accepts an authorized, compatible bound default whose lifecycle is superseded, preserving that exact version pin rather than replacing it with a newer active version. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-289** — CMS-03B-10 missing or concealed default template returns 404 NOT_FOUND without details, template identity, or existence disclosure; the public response is uniform across both cases and leaves no partial create effects. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-290** — CMS-03B-10 an incompatible default template returns 422 VALIDATION_FAILED with SQL DETAIL exactly `["/contentTypeVersionId"]`, using the existing public field-error mapping without template metadata or a new request operand. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-291** — CMS-03B-10 a retired or blocked default template returns 409 CONFLICT without inventing a template-specific public reason; the create UI renders DEC-202's neutral setup copy and does not expose template identities. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-292** — CMS-03B-10 completed exact replay resolves before fresh schema/template dependency validation: after the original default is withdrawn, the same completed request still returns the original canonical entry/revision resource with no duplicate effects; changed payload or scope does not gain replay authority. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-293** — CMS-03B-10 ordinary authorized author-only and editor-only callers can create with the schema-derived default without designer capability or direct registry-read permission; real Auth/context/membership and both authorization stores prove least privilege while creator metadata and caller assignment remain correct. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-294** — CMS-03B-10 locks the complete authorized relation-target/caller-assignment set at position 2 in ascending target UUID order, then the exact default template at position 3, then the content-type version at position 4. Deterministic concurrent tests prove authority/visibility and exact schema/default are rechecked after waits, with no invisible/foreign definition lock lever or late position-2 acquisition. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-295** — CMS-03B-10 default changes affect only later entry creation. Existing immutable revisions keep their original pin or SQL NULL; append, conflict resolution, restore, and locale carry do not initialize or silently rebind historical pins from the current default. [BE03b](../../../wiki/specs/be/03b-editorial-workflow-publication.md) §§Initial revision default template, Write-path lock order and authority fencing; [FE03](../../../wiki/specs/fe/03-cms-content-modeling.md) §Initial entry setup and recovery
- [ ] **P2-S12-AC-296** — CMS-03B-10 an injected reached late unexpected fault propagates as an unexpected failure and atomically rolls back entry, revision, values, assignment, event, evidence, refcount, and idempotency effects. Retrying the same key after rollback produces exactly one complete create, without blanket template-error relabeling.
